<script>
	$(document).on('click', '.industries-menu .industry-title', function () {

    var currentItem = $(this).closest('.industry-item');

    /* Close other industries */
    currentItem
        .siblings('.industry-item')
        .removeClass('active');

    /* Toggle current industry */
    currentItem.toggleClass('active');

});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const menuWrappers = document.querySelectorAll('.im-menu-wrapper');

            menuWrappers.forEach(function (menuWrapper) {
                const categoryItems = menuWrapper.querySelectorAll('.im-category-item');
                const categoryPanels = menuWrapper.querySelectorAll('.im-category-panel');
                const closeBtn = menuWrapper.querySelector('.im-close-btn');

                function switchCategory(item) {
                    const targetId = item.getAttribute('data-target');

                    categoryItems.forEach(function (el) {
                        el.classList.remove('im-active');
                    });
                    categoryPanels.forEach(function (panel) {
                        panel.classList.remove('im-active');
                    });

                    item.classList.add('im-active');
                    const targetPanel = menuWrapper.querySelector('#' + targetId);
                    if (targetPanel) {
                        targetPanel.classList.add('im-active');
                    }
                }

                categoryItems.forEach(function (item) {
                    item.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
                        switchCategory(item);
                    });
                    item.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        switchCategory(item);
                    });
                });

                if (closeBtn) {
                    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        e.stopPropagation();

                        // 1. Max Mega Menu jQuery API support (if used)
                        if (window.jQuery) {
                            const $megaMenu = window.jQuery(menuWrapper).closest('.mega-menu');
                            if ($megaMenu.length && $megaMenu.data('maxmegamenu')) {
                                $megaMenu.data('maxmegamenu').hideAllPanels();
                            }
                        }

                        // 2. Remove active/open classes from all parent Mega Menu plugin wrappers
                        const activeClasses = [
                            'mega-toggle-on',
                            'ubermenu-active',
                            'open',
                            'is-open',
                            'active',
                            'is-active',
                            'show',
                            'sub-menu-open',
                            'menu-item-open',
                            'e-active',
                            'hover',
                            'focus'
                        ];

                        let parentEl = menuWrapper.parentElement;
                        while (parentEl && parentEl !== document.body) {
                            activeClasses.forEach(function (cls) {
                                parentEl.classList.remove(cls);
                            });
                            if (parentEl.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
                                parentEl.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                            }
                            parentEl = parentEl.parentElement;
                        }

                        // 3. Force-hide the sub-menu container (handles CSS :hover mega menus while mouse is inside)
                        const subMenuContainer = menuWrapper.closest(
                            '.mega-sub-menu, .sub-menu, .ubermenu-submenu, .e-n-menu-content, .quadmenu-dropdown-menu, .wp-megamenu-sub-menu, .dropdown-menu, .megamenu-panel'
                        ) || menuWrapper;

                        const parentMenuItem = menuWrapper.closest(
                            'li.mega-menu-item, li.menu-item, li.ubermenu-item, .e-n-menu-item, .quadmenu-item'
                        );

                        subMenuContainer.classList.add('im-force-hide-menu');

                        // 4. Restore the menu automatically once mouse leaves or top nav item is clicked/hovered again
                        const restoreMenu = function () {
                            subMenuContainer.classList.remove('im-force-hide-menu');
                        };

                        if (parentMenuItem) {
                            parentMenuItem.addEventListener('mouseleave', restoreMenu, { once: true });
                            parentMenuItem.addEventListener('click', restoreMenu, { once: true });

                            const navTriggers = parentMenuItem.querySelectorAll('a, button, .mega-menu-link, .e-n-menu-title');
                            navTriggers.forEach(function (trigger) {
                                if (trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
                                    trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                                }
                            });
                        } else {
                            menuWrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', restoreMenu, { once: true });
                        }

                        // 5. Dispatch Escape key & outside click for JS-driven Mega Menu plugins
                        document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Escape', keyCode: 27, bubbles: true }));
                        document.body.click();
                    });
                }
            });
        });

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
        var activeJobTabs = $('.job-areas ul li.selected');
        var tab = activeJobTabs.data('country');
        var prevTab = 'india';
        let $allJobs = $('#' + tab + ' .all-jobs-list-wrap');
        let filteredJobs = $allJobs;

        function scrollToDiv(className, value, time = 500) {
            console.log('classname', className);
             console.log('value', value);
            $('html, body').animate({
                scrollTop: $(className).offset().top - value
            }, time);
        }

        function fetchTigerIndiaJobs(page = 1, searchVal = '') {
            $.ajax({
                url: tigerAjax.ajax_url,
                type: 'POST',
                data: {
                    action: 'get_tiger_india_jobs',
                    page: page,
                    title: searchVal
                },
                beforeSend: function() {
                    scrollToDiv("#india", 180, 200);
                },
                success: function(response) {
                    $('#india .results').html(response);
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                    $('#india .results').html('<div class="jobs-count">There are no openings currently.</div>');
                }
            });
        }

        function showJobs(jobCountry, jobsList, page) {

            let totalJobs = jobsList.length;
            let totalPages = Math.max(Math.ceil(totalJobs / 10), 1);

            let start = (page - 1) * 10;
            let end = start + 10;

            scrollToDiv('#' + jobCountry, 180, 200);
            $allJobs.hide();
            jobsList.slice(start, end).each(function(index) {
                $(this).show();
                $(this).find('.jobs-sl-no').text(start + index + 1);
            });

            if (jobsList.length === 0) {
                $('#' + jobCountry + ' .error').show();
                $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination').hide();
            } else {
                $('#' + jobCountry + ' .error').hide();
                $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination').show();
            }

            // Update pagination UI
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .current_page').text(('0' + page).slice(-2));
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .total_page').text('of ' + ('0' + totalPages).slice(-2));
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination_prev').data('page', page - 1);
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination_next').data('page', page + 1);

            // Show/hide pagination buttons
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination_prev').toggle(page > 1);
            $('#' + jobCountry + ' .career_pagination_next').toggle(page < totalPages);
        }
        $(document).on('click', '#career_other_pagination .page', function(e) {
            var $this = $(this);
            var careerParent = $this.closest('.career_pagination');
            let totalJobs = careerParent.data('tj');
            let totalPages = careerParent.data('tp');
            var jobCountry = (careerParent.data('jc')).toLowerCase();
            let currentPage = $this.data('page');

            if ($this.hasClass('career_pagination_prev')) {
                if (currentPage >= 1) {
                    showJobs(jobCountry, filteredJobs, currentPage);
                }
            }
            if ($this.hasClass('career_pagination_next')) {
                if (currentPage <= totalPages) {
                    showJobs(jobCountry, filteredJobs, currentPage);
                }
            }
        });
        $(document).on('click', '#career_in_pagination .page', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            let page = $(this).data('page');
            var searchVal = $('[name="search_in_jobs"]').val().trim();
            fetchTigerIndiaJobs(page, searchVal);
        });

        $(document).on('click', '.search_box_in .submit', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var searchVal = $('[name="search_in_jobs"]').val().trim();
            var errorElement = $('.search_box_in .error');
            var activeJobTabs = $('.job-areas ul li.selected');
            var tab = activeJobTabs.data('country');
            errorElement.hide();

            if (tab === 'india') {
                if (searchVal.length == 0) {
                    $('.search_box_in .error').show();
                    fetchTigerIndiaJobs(1, '');
                } else {
                    fetchTigerIndiaJobs(1, searchVal);
                }
            } else if (tab === 'us' || tab === 'ca' || tab === 'gb') {
                let keyword = searchVal.toLowerCase();

                if (keyword === '') {
                    filteredJobs = $allJobs;
                    $('.search_box_in .error').show();
                } else {
                    filteredJobs = $allJobs.filter(function() {
                        let title = $(this).data('job-title')?.toLowerCase() || '';
                        return title.includes(keyword);
                    });                   
                }
                showJobs(tab, filteredJobs, 1);
            }
        });
        $(document).on('click', '.search_box_in .reset', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $('[name="search_in_jobs"]').val('');
            var activeJobTabs = $('.job-areas ul li.selected');
            var tab = activeJobTabs.data('country');
            if (tab === 'india') {
                fetchTigerIndiaJobs(1, '');
            } else if (tab === 'us' || tab === 'ca' || tab === 'gb') {
                filteredJobs = $allJobs = $('#' + tab + ' .all-jobs-list-wrap');
                showJobs(tab, filteredJobs, 1);
            }
        });
        $('.job-areas ul li').click(function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $('[name="search_in_jobs"]').val('');
            if (prevTab === 'india') {
                fetchTigerIndiaJobs(1, '');
            } else if (prevTab === 'us' || prevTab === 'ca' || prevTab === 'gb') {
                filteredJobs = $allJobs = $('#' + prevTab + ' .all-jobs-list-wrap');
                showJobs(prevTab, filteredJobs, 1);
            }
            var tab = prevTab = $(this).data('country');

            $('.job-areas ul li').removeClass('selected');
            $('.job_rcon .job_rtab').hide();

            $(this).addClass('selected');
            $('.job_rcon ' + '#' + tab).show();
            filteredJobs = $allJobs = $('#' + tab + ' .all-jobs-list-wrap');
            (tab === 'australia' || tab === 'singapore') ? $('.search_box_in').hide(): $('.search_box_in').show();
        });
    });
    $(".job-areas-slider").slick({
        dots: false,
        infinite: true,
        centerMode: false,
        slidesToShow: 3,
        slidesToScroll: 1,
        loop: true,
        autoplay: false,
        arrows: true,
        variableWidth: true,
        speed: 2000,
        nextArrow: '<button class="slide-arrow next-arrow"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="115.698" height="31.112" viewBox="0 0 115.698 31.112"><g id="Group_30717" data-name="Group 30717" transform="translate(-1664.302 -4292.586)"><text id="SWIPE" transform="translate(1710 4315)" fill="#312e2d" font-size="24" font-family="ArticulatCF-Regular, Articulat CF"><tspan x="0" y="0">SWIPE</tspan></text><g id="Group_30716" data-name="Group 30716" transform="translate(4)"><g id="Group_30715" data-name="Group 30715" transform="translate(208.996 44.293)"><g id="Group_30713" data-name="Group 30713" transform="translate(1485 4263.971) rotate(135)"><path id="Path_63" data-name="Path 63" d="M24,24" transform="translate(-11.14 -10.967)" fill="none" stroke="#f7901d" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="2"/><path id="Path_64" data-name="Path 64" d="M0,8H8V0" transform="translate(4.86 5.033)" fill="none" stroke="#f7901d" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="2"/></g><g id="Group_30714" data-name="Group 30714" transform="translate(1488 4263.971) rotate(135)"><path id="Path_63-2" data-name="Path 63" d="M24,24" transform="translate(0.86 1.033)" fill="none" stroke="#f7901d" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="2"/><path id="Path_64-2" data-name="Path 64" d="M0,20H20V0" transform="translate(4.86 5.033)" fill="none" stroke="#f7901d" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="2"/></g></g></g></g></svg></button>',
    });
    $(".about_tiger").slick({
        dots: false,
        infinite: true,
        centerMode: false,
        slidesToShow: 1,
        slidesToScroll: 1,
        loop: true,
        autoplay: false,
        arrows: true,
        speed: 1000,
    });
</script>

<div class="recruitment-fraud-advisory">
<p>Tiger Analytics has identified fraudulent recruitment communications being circulated by unauthorized individuals impersonating our company using fake domains and forged offer letters.</p>
<p style="margin-bottom: 12px;"><strong>Please be advised:</strong></p>
<ol>
<li>All official communication from Tiger Analytics is sent only through authorized company email domains (@tigeranalytics.com).</li>
<li>Tiger Analytics does not ask candidates for payment, deposits, training fees, or financial information at any stage of the recruitment process.</li>
<li>Candidates are encouraged to verify suspicious emails, interview requests, or offer letters through our official website and careers channels.</li>
</ol>
<p>We are actively working with the relevant authorities and service providers to address this issue.</p>
<p class="m-0">If you receive suspicious communication claiming to be from Tiger Analytics, please report it immediately to <a style="color: #F28913;" href="mailto:careers@tigeranalytics.com">careers@tigeranalytics.com</a></p>

		jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
			function moveDiv() {
				// Move the div after the last p element
				var divElement = $('#perspective_banner .blog-content-main .cs-content-right');
				var pElements = $('#perspective_banner .blog-content-main p');

				if (pElements.length > 0) {
					var lastPElement = pElements.last();
					lastPElement.after(divElement);
				}
			}

			// Initial move for mobile view
			if ($(window).width() <= 767) {
				moveDiv();
			}

			// Move the div when the window is resized to mobile view
			/*$(window).resize(function() {
				if ($(window).width() <= 767) {
					moveDiv();
				}
			});*/
		});

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    document.querySelectorAll('.footr-btm-cta').forEach(function (cta) {
            cta.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (!e.target.closest('a')) {
                    this.querySelector('a')?.click();
                }
            });
        });

     setTimeout(function () {
        const firstH3 = document.querySelector('.vh-section-outer .tab_container h3');

        if (firstH3) {
            firstH3.click();
        }

        const first2H3 = document.querySelector('#cloud-partners .tab_container h3');

        if (first2H3) {
            first2H3.click();
        }
    }, 300);

});

</script>

    // Your Js
$('.tiger-15-btn').each(function () {
    const buttonValue = $(this).attr('value') || $(this).text().trim();
    const href = $(this).closest('a.t15-cta').attr('href');

    if (!buttonValue || !href) {
        $(this).closest('a.t15-cta').hide();
    }
});
